# References inside the bundled jar that do not resolve against the jar or the JDK.
# ITBundledJar fails on any reference missing from this list, so a shade change that
# drops a class or picks a different build of one shows up as a test failure rather
# than as NoClassDefFoundError or NoSuchMethodError in a user's deployment.
#
# Format: class, or class.method(descriptor), or class.field:descriptor, in JVM internal
# form. Add a line only when the referring code is unreachable from RunSync and
# RunCatalogSync; the test prints lines that resolve again so they can be removed.
